Vanguard International Semi.
Valuation
Our target price for VIS of NT$185 is based on 25x 2027E P/E, which we see as justified by its robust PMIC sales growth
momentum and increasing outflow order opportunity from TSMC with support from its consistent cash dividend policy. Our
target price equates to 2026/27E P/B of 3.6x/3.5x.
Risks
Key downside risks to our target price include: 1) weaker 8-inch demand along with higher costs due to inflation or more severe

ASP competition from Chinese peers; 2) lower UTR of the new capacity at the newly acquired fab, with depreciation burden;
and 3) less-than-expected outsourcing opportunities from IDMs.
NXP Semiconductors NV
(NXPI.O; US$238.08; 1; 25 Sep 26; 16:00)
Valuation
Our price target of $370 is based on a 17x P/E applied to CY28 EPS, given peer multiple expansion tied to rising data center
exposure.
Risks
End Market: NXP derives roughly 55% of sales from the automotive end market and roughly 18% of sales from the industrial

end market. Therefore, any major changes in auto sales or industrial activity could result in upside/downside to our estimates
and target price for NXP.
Competition: NXP competes with several companies in analog, digital, and microcontrollers. Consequently, any changes in
market share could result in upside/downside risk to our estimates and target price.
Inventory Risk: NXP sells roughly 50-60% of its products through distributors. As a result, we believe any major fluctuations of

inventory in the distribution channel could result in upside/downside risk to our estimates and target price.
Macroeconomic: NXP’s geographic exposure spans multiple geographies including the U.S., Europe, and Asia. As a result, any
prolonged macroeconomic strength/weakness could result in upside/downside to our estimates and target price.
TSMC
(2330.TW; NT$2475.0; 1; 24 Sep 26; 13:30)
Valuation
Our target price for TSMC shares of NT$3,800 is based on 25x the average of our 2027E EPS, higher than its three-year

average forward PER, versus global peers’ average of 15-23x. We believe our PER target is justified by TSMC's leading position
in advanced process node and robust AI demand growth outlook. Given its continuing leadership in the semiconductor
foundry industry, we believe TSMC’s stable order visibility and earnings outlook make it less vulnerable than peers to a
potential global economic downturn. Our target price equates to 2026E/27E P/E of 34x/25x and 2026E/27E P/B of 13x/10x.

Risks
Key downside risks that could impede the shares from achieving our target price include: 1) weakness in the global
semiconductor market; 2) larger-than-expected margin contractions due to depreciation cost hikes and strong NTD; 3)
competitors entering the foundry business; 4) longer-than-expected digestion of inventory in the supply chain; and 5) a
slowdown in demand either from a global economic downturn or a global trade disruption triggered by tariffs.

Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.
Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk

rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment
rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered

stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating
and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also

suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price
chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in

exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the
expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility

or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:
Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside

call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is
moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day

period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be
different from and does not affect a stock’s fundamental equity rating, which reflects a longer-term total absolute return

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

ab 28 September 2026
Global Research
Nan Ya Plastics
Buy on CCL price hikes and AI-led mix upgrade
Assume coverage with a  Buy and a higher price target of NT$300.00
We assume coverage of Nan Ya Plastics (NYP)  with a Buy and PT of NT$300.  The share
price is up c310% YTD, while Nan Ya PCB (NYPCB),  61% owned by NYP, is up c420%
YTD.  We believe most of NYP's share price appreciation has been driven by the value of
its listed holdings rather than its core Copper Clad Laminate (CCL) business. We

continue to flag earnings upside potential from CCL, driven by ASP increases of
90%/85% in 2026/27E and operating margin expansion from 4.1% in 2025 to
23%/30% in 2026/27E (H126 at c17%). Key drivers include supply led price hikes, a
richer AI product mix and NYP's integrated CCL value chain. In petrochemicals, we
expect the MEG-ethylene spread to turn positive in Q326, with 2026-27E spreads aided
by geopolitical supply tightness. Our 2026/27E earnings are 15%/23% above

Bloomberg consensus. Further upstream tightness and high-end AI CCL order wins are
the upside catalysts.
CCL ASP up 90%/85% in 2026/27E
We expect CCL ASP to rise 90%/85% YoY in 2026/27E:  c80%/55% from supply led
price hikes (see UBS  Tech team's CCL sector report) and c10%/30% from product mix
upgrades. Conventional CCL prices should be supported by AI capacity crowding out
commodity grades, higher material intensity and limited near-term upstream additions.

Industry CCL prices are up 100% in 2026 YTD;  part should flow into 2027E as contracts
reset. We expect M6+ revenue contribution to rise from 8% of CCL revenue in Q326 to
25% in 2027E with ASPs 3-10x higher than conventional FR4 products.
Vertical integration and ASP increase to support 23-30% CCL OPM
NYP's CCL chain covers  glass cloth, epoxy resin and copper foil. We view epoxy resin and
copper foil as fully self-supplied and most glass cloth as internally sourced. This should

support value capture as CCL prices rise. Based on historical sensitivity, we estimate that
a US$1.00 increase in CCL ASP would translate to an incremental US$0.45/0.37 per
piece increase in operating profit in 2026/27E. As a result, we forecast CCL OPM of
23%/30% in 2026/27. In-house material R&D could accelerate AI CCL qualification,
while the partnership with Nittobo would secure Gen2 low-Dk glass cloth and support
the ramp of high-end products.

Valuation:  new price target of NT$300.00
We value NYP using SOTP:  the UBS PT of NT$1,150 for NYPCB; 19x 2027E EV/EBITDA
for non-PCB electronic materials (CCL and upstream); 10x 2027E EV/EBITDA for
petchem (chemicals, plastics and polyester), all in line with peers. Listed stakes (FPCC,
FPC, FCFC, NYT, Mai Liao Power and others)  at UBS's PTs, market values, or peer
multiples. After a 10% HoldCo discount, our PT is NT$300.00, implying 15x 2027E PE,

0.3x PEG and 4.4x P/BV.

Thesis Map     UBS                    Research     THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q: Will  CCL and electronic materials (EM) become the  key profit drivers?
Yes. We expect EM revenue to rise from 46% in 2025 to 59%/69% in 2026/27E, and to account for
nearly all EBIT. Within EM, CCL would be the growth engine, up from 24% of EM revenue  in 2025 to

31%/43% in 2026/27E.
Q: Will the conventional CCL upcycle last through 2027?
Yes. UBS's Tech team expects conventional CCL prices and profits to keep rising in 2027E on: 1) high-
end AI grades taking  capacity from commodity CCL;  2) higher material intensity  in AI-CCL lifting
upstream demand; and 3) tight weaving capacity limiting near-term capacity additions.
Q: Can NYP achieve higher volume in AI-CCL
Yes. Vertical integration would likely help "recipe" changes and customer qualification. The Nittobo

tie-up should secure low-Dk glass and support high-end ramp.
We assume coverage of NYP with a Buy rating and a PT of NT$300.00. The share price is up c310%
YTD, while NYPCB, 61%-owned by NYP, is up c420% YTD. We think most of the share price
movement reflects its listed holdings rather than a full re-rating of the core CCL business. We
continue to expect earnings upside from CCL ASP growth of 90%/85% and CCL OPM expansion
from 4.1% in 2025 to 23%/30% in 2026/27E (H126 at c17%) , driven by supply led price hikes, a

richer AI mix and its integrated CCL value chain. For petchem, we expect the MEG-ethylene spread to
turn positive in Q326E, with 2026/27E margins supported by geopolitical led supply tightness.
Further upstream tightness and high-end AI CCL order wins are the key upside catalysts.
1) Conventional CCL prices are up nearly 100% YTD vs. 2025;  hikes continue to occur on a monthly
basis. 2) CCL and glass cloth are running at close to full capacity (c90% utilisation). An increase in

capacity  of 6.2m pcs/month in Q326 to 6.5m by Q426; 6.7-7.0m by end-2027 according to the
company. 3) M7 and M8 low-/ultra low-loss CCL products are now listed on the company's website
(updated in September 2026). 4) The November 2025 partnership with Nittobo should help the
company secure next-gen low-Dk glass yarn supply  for high-end AI CCL.
NYP is trading at 12x 2027E PE and 3.6x 2027E P/BV, significantly below EM peers at 36x 2027E PE
and 8.2x P/BV.  Our PT of NT$300.00 implies 15x 2027E PE and 4.4x 2027E P/BV. We believe the gap

reflects concerns about petchem, rather than improved CCL profitability.

Nan Ya Plastics one of the core companies of Taiwan's Formosa Group. Its businesses span electronic
materials, chemicals, plastics processing and polyester. NYP is one of the largest CCL producers, with

a vertically integrated CCL value chain, supplying CCL to its 61%-owned subsidiary Nan Ya PCB and
other printed circuit board (PCB) makers. It owns 29% of Nan Ya Tech, the only Taiwan-based
dynamic random access memory (DRAM) brand. It also owns 23% of Formosa Petrochemical.

Nan Ya Plastics 28 September 2026 ab 3
Nan Ya Plastics UBS Research
NYP CCL: a vertically integrated traditional CCL leader; AI
CCL ramping
Copper foil, epoxy resin and glass fibre are the three major materials for CCL, accounting
for 42%, 26% and 19% of conventional CCL costs, respectively, according to
Trendforce. As CCL migrates to higher end AI grades, we expect glass fibre and specialty
resin to take a larger share of total BOM cost, mainly driven by higher value low-Dk glass

and low-loss resin.
NYP has a highly vertically integrated CCL supply chain with in-house production
spanning glass yarn, glass fibre, epoxy resin, copper foil, and CCL. We believe vertical
integration differentiates NYP from other CCL companies given: 1) Provides supply
security: during periods of tight raw material supply, NYP can rely on internal key
material capacity, thereby reducing procurement risk and supporting stable production.
2) Faster customer qualification : with key upstream materials produced in-house,

NYP can adjust formulations and specifications more quickly to meet customer-specific
performance requirements during high-end CCL qualification. 3) Better cost control:
internal sourcing allows NYP to capture more value across the supply chain.

AI bandwidth growth boosts high-end CCL demand
As AI chips become more powerful ( Blackwell to NVIDIA's Rubin and Vera Rubin ), the
chips need to send more data at much faster speeds. The faster the data travels, the
easier it is for the signal to weaken or lose quality.  This means AI servers need better

circuit board materials to keep data moving quickly and reliably. High-end CCL, such as
M8 and M9, reduces this signal loss and therefore is becoming increasingly important as
AI computing power and data speeds continue to rise.

Vertical integration supports higher OPM
We compared CCL profitability with KBL (Kingboard Laminates, 1888.HK; covered by
UBS analyst Edward Liu), NYP and ITEQ (ITEQ Corporation, 6213.TW; Not Covered) and

note that vertical integration appears to support a higher margin. Fully integrated across
the three key CCL upstream materials, KBL delivered a 26% OPM in H126, followed by
17% for NYP,   and 11% for ITEQ which primarily sources upstream materials externally.
While product mix differs across peers, we believe companies with vertical integrated
supply chains are able to deliver better cost controls for raw materials and thus capture
higher value across the supply chain, particularly as upstream prices rise.

Supply-driven price upcycle to continue
Despite lukewarm consumer electronics demand, conventional CCL remains in a strong
pricing upcycle. The company has said that prices for mid and low-end CCL continue to
rise 5-10% per month, while certain grades are recording monthly increases of 10-15%.

The current supply shortfall reflects a combination of the following:
1) Capacity migration to high-end AI materials. As industry companies increasingly
prioritise AI-related products, a growing portion of CCL capacity is being reallocated
from conventional grades. High-end materials also require significantly longer
processing times and lower throughput, effectively reducing industry-wide supply of
traditional CCL.
2) Rising material intensity and order spillover. Increasing PCB complexity, including

higher layer counts in AI servers and networking equipment, is structurally raising CCL
consumption per unit. Concurrently, leading high-end CCL suppliers are focusing on
premium products, resulting in order spillover into the conventional CCL market and
tightening supply further.
3) Persistent upstream bottlenecks.  Tight key raw material supply, particularly for
glass fibre cloth and copper foil, continues to constrain effective capacity additions. The

limited availability of electronic grade glass cloth, long lead times for weaving
equipment and emerging shortages in high-end copper foil have extended the supply
crunch across the broader CCL value chain.
As the AI cycle continues, UBS's Tech team expects industry supply-demand conditions
to remain tight, at least through 2027E as capacity expansion requires multiple years to
materialise and is unlikely to keep pace with demand growth, in our view. As a result, we

expect conventional CCL pricing to remain on an upward trajectory, supporting further
margin expansion across the traditional CCL value chain.

Supply-driven price hikes to drive 80%/55% ASP growth in 2026/27E
Conventional CCL pricing has entered a sharp supply-driven upcycle. According to

TPCA,  Kingboard's FR4 price hikes exceeded 50% cumulatively by July, while local media
estimated YTD increases had exceeded 100% by end-August following further hikes.
NYP also confirmed 100% YTD price hikes for a number of conventional CCL products.
According to the Panasonic Industry Official Price Announcement , Panasonic
announced  three rounds of price increases for general glass-epoxy CCL in 2026 of 30%,
20% and 30%, equivalent to 103% on a compounded basis.

NYP management noted that the 26Q4 pricing upcycle remains intact, with CCL prices
rising 5-10% monthly, while some grades have increased more than 100% cumulatively
since 2025. KBL also confirmed that conventional CCL prices have more than doubled
versus end-2025. KBL raised prices twice in April and accelerated monthly increases to
15% in June and July.
Historically, NYP's CCL ASP has moved in the same direction as KBL. Based on the UBS
Tech team's sector view, we forecast CCL ASP price increases of 80%/55% in 2026/27,

driven purely by pricing. We flag the announced industry price hikes of up to 100% are
not immediately fully reflected in realised ASP, as implementation varies by customer and
often requires contract renewal or price renegotiation.

EMC anticipates undersupply to continue in the next two years
At the UBS Taiwan Summit, EMC (2383.TW, Not Covered) anticipated further price hikes
in H226 based on rising component costs, but that the magnitude of the increase would
likely be less than in H126. Management noted equipment lead time currently is one to
two years and that high-end CCL demand has not slowed. It thus anticipates

undersupply to continue in the next two years.
High-end CCL: next-gen AI rack architecture to lift CCL content and ASP
High-end AI CCL is mainly used in AI server compute boards, switch boards and
midplane/backplanes, where higher data transmission speeds require low-loss laminate
materials. Using NVIDIA as an example as its rack architecture is increasingly PCB/CCL
intensive, the current VR200 system is built on an Oberon NVL72 rack design, while

### Page 9

Nan Ya Plastics 28 September 2026 ab 9
future generations could adopt the Kyber rack, which would increase the NVLink
domain per rack to 144 or above GPU.
Compared with current Oberon architecture, Kyber should increase CCL content per
rack as it supports twice or above GPUs and relies on increasingly complex, high layer
count PCB interconnects. This should drive both higher CCL volume and a richer mix of
advanced low-loss materials, supporting higher CCL value per rack. High-end CCL share

matters disproportionately for revenue because M8+ ASP can be several times higher
than conventional FR4.
While our base case assumes continued adoption of higher-end AI CCL, supply-chain
feedback suggests Nvidia's Kyber rack architecture may be delayed beyond Rubin Ultra,
potentially extending the use of the current Oberon platform through much of 2028.
This could defer part of the expected AI-related materials upgrade cycle. However, we
see a potential offset from ASIC programs, with Google TPU v9 and Amazon Trainium 4

expected to transition from 2-die to 4-die architectures from late 2027. The resulting
increase in bandwidth requirements and system complexity could support incremental
demand for PCB/CCL materials, partially mitigating the impact of a slower Kyber
transition.
AI CCL product mix upgrade to drive 10%/30% ASP expansion in
2026/27E
We expect M6+ CCL volume to increase from 0% in 2025 to 2% in 2026E and 5% in
2027E. We think this would be supported by: 1) NYP starting M7/8 CCL shipments,

while M9/10 are undergoing customer qualification, according to a news report; 2) the
company is ramping up capacity to meet increasing demand (Figure 5); and 3) improving
low-Dk glass supply through its partnership with Nittobo.
We model M6&M6+ ASP at 3-10x higher than conventional FR4; we forecast the
expansion in high-end CCL volume would result in a 10% and 30% YoY increases in
blended ASP.

Partnership with Nittobo to strengthen NYP's position in high-end AI
CCL
Nittobo is a leading supplier of specialty electronic glass fibre cloth for advanced AI and

high-speed applications. According to TrendForce, it holds approximately 90% of the
global T-glass market and 60-70% of the next-generation low-Dk glass market, while
Nittobo describes its NE-glass (low dielectric specialty glass) as the de facto standard for
low-Dk applications.
In November 2025, Nittobo and NYP announced a strategic partnership under which
Nittobo will provide NYP with a long term and stable supply of next-gen low-Dk glass
yarn (the upstream material for glass cloth), while NYP will provide specialty glass cloth

weaving capacity by utilising the company's 4,000 weaving machine capacity.
We expect the partnership to accelerate NYP's move into high-end AI CCL, as it secures
access to a scare upstream material, reduces supply bottleneck risk, and should improve
the company's ability to develop and qualify higher end low-loss CCL products.
Figure 15: NYP's partnership with Nittobo in AI-specialty glass fibre cloth for

We expect CCL OPM to rise to 23%/30% in 2026/27E
Historically, a US$1.00 per piece increase in CCL ASP results in a US$0.23/0.43 rise in
CCL operating profit per piece. Based on this, we estimate that a US$1.00 ASP increase
would translate to a  US$0.45 per piece incremental OP in 2026E, as price hikes and
operating leverage allow a larger portion of ASP gains to translate to profit. We expect

### Page 11

Nan Ya Plastics 28 September 2026 ab 11
flow through to moderate in 2027E at $0.37, as ASP growth is increasingly driven by
higher end CCL products, which have a lower scale compared to conventional products
and higher raw material costs.

Comparison with consensus. Our 2026E/27 net profit forecasts are 15%/23% above
consensus. This is primarily driven by our EBIT estimates, which are 21%/30% above
consensus as we expect 90%/85% increases in CCL ASP in 2026/27E to significantly
expand OP margins. As a result, we expect the company's CCL OPM to rise from c4% in

2025 to 23%/30% in 2026/27E, underpinned by higher ASP and the company’s cost
control capabilities within its vertically integrated supply chain.
In addition, our 2026E/27 investment income forecasts are 10%/23% higher than
consensus, largely reflecting higher equity income contributions from Nanya Technology
(NYT, 2408.TW, Buy, covered by UBS analyst Nicolas Gaudois), in which the company
holds a 29% stake.
Comparison with historical valuation. The stock is currently trading at 14x P/E based

on 12-month forward consensus estimates, 1SD below its five-year historical average
PE. It trades at  3.9x P/BV on 12-month forward consensus estimates, supported by a
21% 12-month forward ROE. Both P/BV and ROE are at five-year historical highs.
NYP owns a 61% equity stake in NYPCB (8046.TW), a 29% stake in NYT (2408.TW),
23% of FPCC (Formosa Petrochemical Corporation, 6505.TW), 4.6% of FPC (Formosa
Plastics Corporation, 1301.TW) and 2.4% of FCFC (Formosa Chemicals & Fibre

Corporation, 1326.TW). Excluding the market value of these listed companies and
assuming a stable holdco discount over time, we view the residual value as representing
NYP’s core operations  (CCL and the upstream business, as well as the company’s
petchem business). NYP's implied core business currently accounts for 33% of its market
cap, approximately 1SD above its historical average. However, we estimate CCL and
petrochemicals will contribute c30% of earnings in 2027E and c50% by 2030E. We

believe the market is undervaluing NYP’s core business, as concerns on its petchem
segment are overshadow the company’s transition toward faster-growing, higher-value
CCL earnings.
Comparison with sector peers. NYP trades at 12x 2027E PE and 3.6x 2027E P/BV on
our estimates, compared to 36x 2027E PE and 8.2x P/BV for its EM peers. We believe the
market undervalues the company’s expanding CCL business due to excessive concerns
about the legacy petchem business that could weigh on margins. We expect continued

geopolitical volatility to support MEG-ethylene spreads and to preserve petchem
profitability from 2026 to H127E.

Valuation: CCL business to drive valuation upside
We value Nan Ya  Plastics using a SOTP  valuation methodology.
Core business. For the company's PCB business, we use the UBS price target of NT
$1,150  (covered by UBS analyst Diana Chang). For non-PCB  EM, we value the business
at 19x 2027E EV/EBITDA, a discount to  industry  CCL upstream peers, reflecting lower
scarcity and pricing power than upstream bottleneck materials, but higher than CCL
companies, supported by more vertical integration and value capture than pure CCL

producers as well as a commitment to shareholder returns via dividend payments (not
less than a 50% payout ratio).  For the company's core chemicals, plastics and polyester
businesses, we assign 10x 2027E EV/EBITDA, in line with Asia petchem  peers.
Investments. For the company's investments in FPCC, FPC, FCFC, NYT, Mai Liao Power
and others,  we assume UBS price targets and market values where relevant for the listed
holdings, or we assume peer valuation multiples.

HoldCo discount. We apply a relatively modest 10% HoldCo discount to NYP, given
their transparent market values, visible dividend policies and consistent cash
distributions. NYP has demonstrated an ability and willingness to monetise the holdings.
In 2026, NYP sold a 19.4m equity stake in NYPCB for NT$13.4bn and also approved the
disposal of 31m NYT shares, demonstrating that these stakes are monetisable. Regular
dividends from the listed companies also allow NYP to realise part of their underlying

value without requiring outright disposal. Our sensitivity applies a 5-30% HoldCo
discount with 10% as our base case.
Our SOTP-derived PT of NT$300.00 implies 15x 2027E PE, 0.3x 27E PEG and a 4.4x P/BV.
We assume coverage of NYP with a Buy rating.

Nan Ya Plastics 28 September 2026 ab 17
Nan Ya Plastics UBS Research
Company Description
Company Description
Established in 1958 and listed on the TAIEX in 1967, Nan Ya
Plastics one of the core companies of Taiwan's Formosa Group.
Its businesses span electronic materials, chemicals, plastics
processing and polyester. NYP is one of the largest copper clad
laminates (CCL) producers, with a vertically integrated CCL value
chain, supplying CCL to its 61%-owned subsidiary Nan Ya PCB

and other printed circuit board (PCB) makers. It owns 29% of
Nan Ya Tech, the only Taiwan-based dynamic random access
memory (DRAM) brand. It also owns 23% of Formosa
Petrochemical.
Industry Outlook
The traditional FR4-alike CCL industry is in a supply-driven
upcycle with further pricing upside as AI continues to crowd out
conventional capacity, while high-end CCL is entering a multi-
year AI-driven growth cycle, led by specification upgrades and

increasing penetration of servers and networking. 2026
feedstock disruptions amid geopolitical tensions have caused
some temporary tightening, but the medium-term outlook
remains weak unless capacity rationalisation accelerates or end-
demand improves.

Forecast returns

Company Description
Established in 1958 and listed on the TAIEX in 1967, Nan Ya Plastics one of the core
companies of Taiwan's Formosa Group. Its businesses span electronic materials, chemicals,
plastics processing and polyester. NYP is one of the largest copper clad laminates (CCL)

producers, with a vertically integrated CCL value chain, supplying CCL to its 61%-owned
subsidiary Nan Ya PCB and other printed circuit board (PCB) makers. It owns 29% of Nan Ya
Tech, the only Taiwan-based dynamic random access memory (DRAM) brand. It also owns
23% of Formosa Petrochemical.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Nan Ya Plastics:
We base our price target for NYP on a SOTP valuation.
Key upside risks include: 1) stronger CCL pricing; 2) faster high-end CCL mix upgrades; and 3)

a faster petchem margin recovery.
Key downside risks are: 1) a CCL pricing reversal on faster supply additions or weaker
demand; 2) slower AI qualification/ramp of the company's CCL business; and 3) further
weakening chemicals/polyester businesses.
Nan Ya Printed Circuit Board:
Our price target for Nan Ya Printed Circuit Board is based on P/E multiple methodology.
Upside risks include faster-than-expected ramp-up of networking/switches, stronger-than-

expected pricing increase, better-than-estimated consumer demand, and continued tightness
in BT substrates.
Downside risks include lower-than-expected ramp-up of networking/switch projects, lower-
than-expected global PC/server demand, oversupply from capacity expansions, raw material
cost increases, intensified competition, and a lack of spec upgrades and content increase.
As for industry risks, substrate applications are dominated by PCs, but servers, artificial

intelligence and advanced computing are driving demand growth. We believe key risks to the
industry include slower-than-expected growth in servers, networking, and high performance
computing, ASP pressure, margin erosion, and lack of spec upgrades. We believe the flexible
circuit board and high-density interconnect board segments are more vulnerable to
fluctuations in the consumer product cycle and have lower visibility.
Nanya Technology:
Nanya Tech is a pure-play DRAM manufacturer based in Taiwan mainly focused on legacy DDR

products for customers across various end applications. We think downside risks to our thesis
could include a potential slowdown in end demand and hence the conventional memory
cycle as well as Nanya's legacy-centric product portfolio. We value Nanya Tech shares using a
target NTM P/BV multiple based on long-term average ROE and cost of equity.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

### Page 2

Gold Circuit Electronics (2368.TW)
29 September 2026 Citi Research
2
Gold Circuit Electronics
Valuation
Our TP of NT$1,630 for GCE is based on 25x our 2027E EPS estimate, which is higher than 22x or +2std level of its 1-year-
forward PE over the past three years. We believe the 25x multiple is justified by GCE's high AI server/networking sales
exposure, market share gain in existing ASIC customer and new AI ASIC customer wins from 4Q26, which drives its AI ASIC

sales growth and margin profile. Recall that TSMC expects close to a 50%+ CAGR in the next five years for its AI-related
demand, which is stronger than any other applications. Thus, we believe investors would be willing to pay for high-growth AI
names (especially ASIC) with high PE multiples. Our target price is equivalent to 44x/25x our 2026/27E EPS estimates and
16.5x/9.9x our 2026/27E BVPS.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target price include: 1) weaker-than-expected server

recovery; 2) slower ramp-up pace of new server platform; 3) less-than-expected AI demand; 4) more PCB peers added into AI
supply chain; and 5) stricter practice of China+1 by customers due to geopolitical concerns.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

Source PDF: all report pdf/260930_daiwa_delta.pdf
Report source: 未公開來源
Date: 2026-09-30

Status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We provide our views on Delta after c.4 months of share price 
weakness, driven by investor concerns on HVDC delays, and a slowdown 
in power consumption trend amid deceleration in Nvidia’s GPU shipment 
growth YoY. We remain positive on Delta over 2026-28E, as rising AI ASIC 
server trend should drive a meaningful increase in DC-DC power module 
ASPs (vs. Nvidia’s discrete form factor). We expect higher HVDC adoption 
from 2028E, driven by IT rack and power supply architecture changes.

What’s the impact: Component shortages are the key near-term 
bottleneck. We attribute the recent share-price weakness to slower-than-
expected revenue momentum in both 2Q26, due to vendor-sanction issue 
at Delta Thailand, and 3Q26E, due to a worsening shortage of power 
semiconductors. We believe the 2Q26 revenue shortfall was a one-off 
event, though 3Q26E growth is likely to remain constrained by the power-
semi shortage. While supply capacity should improve, the supply-demand 
gap will likely persist for some time. That said, end-demand should remain 
sound. Given the continued improvement in revenue momentum, we still 
expect operating margin to reach a record-high of 18.3% in 3Q26.

Long-term growth drivers. We see Delta as a key beneficiary of the AI 
ASIC server trend. We were previously concerned that rising AI ASIC 
adoption could hurt Delta, as ASICs have lower thermal design power 
(TDP) than Nvidia GPUs, but now believe that Delta will benefit from rising 
content value of DC-DC power modules, as hyperscalers are more willing 
to spend on DC-DC modules (vs. discrete) despite higher initial cost given 
their superior energy efficiency. We forecast DC-DC power module revenue 
to reach c.TWD60bn in 2026E (vs. TWD9bn in 2025 and our prior estimate 
of c.TWD20bn for 2026E). This figure could increase further with rising 
ASIC usage in 2027. We also believe HVDC adoption rate could reach at 
least 50% in 2028E (vs. our prior expectation of 30%), as power supply 
architecture is becoming increasingly bulky (to accommodate aluminium 
capacitors inside power supply amid the super capacitor shortage). Limited 
space in IT racks would force customers to adopt more power racks and 
likely equip them with high voltage versions to improve efficiency.

What we recommend: We raise our 2026-28E EPS by 2-15% on higher 
HVDC contribution. We lift our 12M TP to TWD3,088 (from TWD2,988) 
based on a lower target PER of 49x (from 55x) to reflect market sentiment, 
(at the high-end of its past-3-year range of 11-50x) applied to our 1-year-
forward EPS. Key downside risk: lower-than-expected demand.

How we differ: Our 2028E earnings is 21% above the Bloomberg 
consensus, likely due to our more bullish assumption for HVDC adoption.

30 September 2026

Key beneficiary of the AI ASIC server trend

➢ Better-than-expected DC-DC AI server contribution from 2026E 
➢ HVDC adoption rate should be higher-than-expected in 2028E 
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) rating and raising 12M TP to TWD3,088

Company profile 
Founded in 1971, Delta Electronics has been the global leader in switching power supply solutions since 
2002 and DC brushless fans since 2006. Its business covers 3 key areas: power electronics (eg, power 
supplies, thermal management products and passive components), automation (eg, controllers, robot 
arms and building management systems), and infrastructure (eg, datacentre infrastructure, networking 
and EV charging).

### Page 5

5

Delta Electronics (2308 TT): 30 September 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 30 Sep 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 1
Delta's board has 7 non-independent and 5 independent directors with a term of three years.
The total number of directors and ratio of independent directors appear higher than most other
listed companies. Besides, according to Delta's CSR report, enhancing the board's functions is
one of the key goals for the company to achieve better corporate governance; also, the board
members' diversity and professionalism will be taken into consideration to ensure a good board
quality. Moreover, the company evaluates all the directors in the board using internal regulations
and an external independent institution each year and at least once every three years,
respectively. We believe Delta has implemented relatively more enhanced measure than most
listed firms to improve its board quality.
Capital management 2
During 2016-19, Delta's payout ratio was stable in the range of c.70%, but fell to 56% in 2020
and 53% in 2021. For 2020, it was mainly due to a sizeable non-cash re-valuation gain after the
acquisition of Delta Thailand (DET), while the company maintained more cash on hand to deal
with the uncertainties caused by COVID-19. However, the DPS growth from 2016 to 2021 was
limited, as Delta struggled with business diversification into new end-markets (eg, smartphone,
NEV). We believe that Delta's effort started to bear fruit in recent years after its NEV business
has gradually picked up. Overall, we see a stable dividend policy from Delta and expect its DPS
to continue to grow YoY in the future given that they declared a TWD11.6 DPS for 2025 which
equals a 50% payout ratio.
Related party &
transaction 1 Revenue from related parties stood at 12% of total 2025 revenue and purchases from related
parties were at 11%. We see limited risk from related party transactions.
S Supply chain
management 1
Delta focuses on: 1) localised management, 2) supplier certification and risk management, and
3) supplier sustainability engagement as key initiatives under the scope of supply chain
management. In 2019, 144 new suppliers joined Delta's supply chain system. All of them passed
environmental and social evaluations, signed the declaration of "non-use of conflict materials",
and complied with Responsible Business Alliance's (BRA) Code of Conduct. Also, these vendors
had to implement and abide by Delta's supply chain CSR policy and code of conduct. To lower
ESG risks, Delta carries out RBA audits and monitors high-risk critical suppliers each year. The
suppliers that fail these audits have to provide detailed improvement plans in response to
identified issues. Besides, Delta has surveyed a total of 263 suppliers with potential risk and 63
suppliers with high risk of the total 1,331 suppliers, based on its CSR policy and code of conduct.
If these suppliers are flagged as high-risk and no concrete improvements are made, Delta may
consider terminating cooperation and transactions with them to improve the company's
engagement on supplier sustainability. Overall, we think Delta has a solid plan to improve its
supply chain management with specific KPIs, including: 1) achieving supplier improvement rate
of 80% in 2025, 2) reducing GHG emissions rate of critical suppliers by 10% in 2025, and 3)
improving water saving rate of critical suppliers by 12% in 2025.
S Data security 2
Delta's Information Security team created Delta's Information Security Policy in November 2013
and started providing educational training to global employees in 2014. Delta gradually
introduced the Information Security Management System (ISMS) in 2016 and obtained the ISO
27001 international certification in 2018. According to ISO27001, Delta has to conduct at least
one internal audit on information security and appoint an independent institution to conduct audit
externally. According to Delta, no major deficiencies have been found in recent years.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

### Page 1

Summary: Global Unichip (GUC) reported September revenue which closed its 3Q26 top-
line 19% above market expectations thanks to its stronger-than-expected turnkey revenue 
run-rate during the quarter, likely attributable to more capacity support from its key foundry 
helping GUC meet strong demand from customers for AI projects. We see upside risk to 
our/consensus 3Q26 bottom-line estimates but are currently comfortable with our 4Q26 
forecasts despite the 3Q26 revenue beat likely dwarfing QoQ growth for 4Q26. We have a 
Buy (1) rating on the counter and will review our model in due course post its quarterly 
results release.

Highlight 
• 3Q26 top-line beating expectations. GUC released September revenue of TWD7.1bn, 
up 22% MoM (+98% YoY, see chart below), which closed its 3Q26 top-line at 
TWD18.8bn, up 35% QoQ (+118% YoY) for a new record. 3Q26 revenue beat our and 
consensus estimates both by 19% each thanks to its turnkey business (ie, mass-
production) which contributed TWD16.7bn for 89% of total and beat our estimate by 
22%. We attribute the beat to likely its key foundry continuously unleashing extra 
capacity helping GUC meet the strong customer AI demand during the quarter.

• Quarterly results preview and outlook. We expect GUC to have earned 3Q26 net 
profit of TWD1.7bn, varying not much from the consensus estimate, yet given the top-
line beat, we now see upside risk to the market expectations. As for 4Q26, our top-line 
and bottom-line forecasts are currently 10% and 26% above the consensus, 
respectively, making us comfortable with our current forecasts since we see the 3Q26 
beat being driven by increased foundry capacity allocation for some shipment pull-in, 
thus dwarfing 4Q26’s QoQ growth on intact AI demand. As flagged in our previous note 
(Upgrading: unleashed capacity adding to growth, 5 August 2026), we continue to 
expect GUC to be a key beneficiary from the strong AI ASIC ramps, including AI CPU 
and accelerators.

Recommendation 
We rate GUC Buy (1) with a 12M TP of TWD 5,125 on our 4-quarter forward PER target of 
63x. The stock performed well recently which we see as largely pricing in the 3Q26 top-line 
beat, therefore only suggesting investors accumulate only on any technical pullback. Key 
downside risks are less foundry support and margin execution.

Earnings revision: We factor in Landmark 3Q26 revenues and revise up earnings by 5%/ 
1%/ 1%/ 1% in 2026-29E mainly on higher revenues of CW laser epi-wafer and diodes 
for 1.6T products, supported by rising demand from high-speed optical modules. We 
keep GM and Opex ratio largely unchanged.

Valuation: We employ a discounted P/E methodology (unchanged) and apply a 61.5x 
(unchanged, report link) target P/E multiple on our 2029E EPS, discounted back to 
2027E at a COE of 10.5% (beta 1.7x, risk free rate 1.6%, and market risk premium at 
5.1% - unchanged). Our target P/E multiple of 61.5x is derived from the PEG&M ratio of 
peers (unchanged) in the photonics supply chain. Consequently, our 12-month target 
price is now NT$4,459 (vs. previously at NT$4,401). Our new TP implied 2028E P/E of 
76x is between company’s avg. P/E at 62x and avg. +1 P/E at 91x. Maintain Buy.

Valuation: We employ a discounted P/E methodology, applying a 61.5x target P/E

Source:                         Trendforce, Morgan Stanley Research
TV panel prices to stay  flat MoM in October: Mainstream 32", 43", 55", 65" and 75"
TV panel prices are expected to stay flat for the month of October. While panel
makers had previously notified their brands customers of potential price hikes from
4Q26 to mitigate elevated component costs, we believe that negotiations are
ongoing, as TV panel order momentum might slow down in the coming months on

seasonality and TV brands could reduce orders on higher panel prices. On the other
hand, panel makers continue to demonstrate good discipline, since Chinese panel
makers are committed to annual maintenance during the October holiday to control
output. We think it seems more likely that we will see TV panel price hikes toward
late 4Q26 when TV brands start to restock for CNY promotions. Our base-case
assumption is for the TV panel prices  to be flat QoQ in 4Q26, on a quarterly average

basis.
IT panel prices to remain flat MoM in October: Monitor and notebook panel prices
are expected to be flat MoM during the month. Both applications continue to  see
slower demand in  2H26 post the earlier restocking  in 1H26 amid concerns about
increasing component costs. That said, panel makers are under cost pressure and
reluctant to given more price concessions. On a quarterly average basis, we expect
IT panel prices to remain flat QoQ in 4Q26.

Risk/reward  less attractive: We maintain the view that the current valuation of
Taiwanese panel makers, AUO (2409.TW) at 2.0x P/B and Innolux (3481.TW) at 1.7x
P/B, have already baked in the expectations of glass in advanced packaging for
revenue contribution in 2028. However, some supply-chain players think this might
take longer, and there seem to be more suppliers from different supply chains vying
for this opportunity. In China, while expectations are also building for advanced

packaging, we have a    relative preference for BOE (000725.SZ; 1.4x P/B) over TCL
(000100.SZ; 1.7x P/B), on its scale  and faster progress in glass core substrates.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley
Research. Investors should consider Morgan Stanley
Research as only a single factor in making their investment

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
We believe current valuations of 1.5-2.0x P/B have already priced in expectations
of glass in advanced packaging from 2H28 for the panel makers.
Nonetheless,  the risk in commercialization and competition remain key to watch.

TV Panel Prices Trended Down in 3Q26
TV panel prices started to edge up at the beginning of this year, rising 1% MoM in January,
2% MoM in February and 2% MoM in March. April, May and June were largely flat, while
July was down 2% MoM, August was down 1% MoM, and September was flat MoM,   in line
with our expectation that TV panel prices would start trending down from 3Q26.  That
said, we do not expect a significant decline as supply-side discipline should provide

downside support.

Below-seasonal Panel Shipments in 2H26
Over the past decade, TV panel shipments have typically been split 49%:51% between 1H
and 2H. In 2026, we expect the mix to skew toward 1H, as TV brands restocked ahead of
major promotional events, including the Winter Olympics in February, the FIFA World Cup
in June-July, and China's 618 shopping festival. We see no clear evidence that these events

will materially boost global TV demand, but they have historically shifted shipment timing
across quarters. As a result, we expect the restocking momentum seen since the start of
the year to fade, with panel order momentum slowing in 2H26.
Display Fab Utilization  ~85% in 3Q26
Stronger TV panel shipments supported display fab utilization of ~80% in 4Q24 and 82%
in 1Q25. Utilization declined to 78% in 2Q25 as brands adopted a more cautious stance
following earlier pull-ins and as the impact of China's trade-in program moderated.

Utilization rebounded to 80-85% in 3Q25 as brands restocked for year-end promotions
and remained at similar levels in 4Q25 despite seasonal softness as well as in 1Q26.
Industry utilization stayed broadly flat QoQ, at 80-85% in 2Q26, reflecting continued
supply discipline, followed by a slight uptick in 3Q26.
Over the medium to long term, we continue to believe the display industry is undergoing
structural change. Chinese panel makers have shifted their focus from top-line growth to

profitability, while lower government subsidies have reinforced supply discipline. We
believe investors increasingly recognize that the industry's production-to-order model can
remain sustainable, particularly as panel price cycles shortened to quarters in 2023-24 vs.
multiyear cycles in the previous decade. Consolidation actions, including LG Display's sale
of its Gen 8.5 Guangzhou fab to TCL and Sharp's closure of its Gen 10 Sakai fab, also

support a healthier supply backdrop (for more information, see Display – Structural
Changes on Supply Side Not Fairly Priced In, June 17, 2021).

Morgan Stanley Research 5
Risk/Reward Largely Fair
We view risk-reward as broadly balanced following the recent correction in panel stocks.
Below are our current views:
• AUO (2409.TW, Derrick Yang): We rate AUO Equal-weight with a PT of NT
$27.00, based on a 1.4x 2026e P/B multiple. While we believe the current round of
TV panel price increases is nearing an end, major panel makers should continue to
demonstrate solid supply discipline, providing downside support. We appreciate

AUO's efforts to diversify its business, but meaningful earnings contributions are
likely to take time.
• BOE (000725.SZ, Derrick Yang): We prefer BOE due to its scale advantage,
higher exposure to display and faster progress in glass core substrates. Our PT of
Rmb9.30 is based on 2.5x 2026e P/B, which we view as reasonable given our
estimated ROE of 5-8% over 2026-28 compared with BOE's average P/B of 1.1x and
ROE of 2-4% during 2023-25.
• Innolux (3481.TW, Derrick Yang): In semiconductors, our supply-chain checks

suggest that Innolux is participating in a foundry customer's glass core substrate
project. However, we do not expect meaningful contributions before 2028, and
additional competitors could enter as the industry moves toward mass production.
We maintain our Equal-weight rating and PT of NT$60.00, based on a 2.1x 2026e
P/B multiple.
• LG Display (034220.KS, Ryan Kim): Meaningful OLED revenue contribution
should increasingly improve earnings from 2H25, but potentially weaker end-

markets keep us from turning bullish on LGD. The stock trades at 0.6x 2026e P/B
vs. a historical mid-cycle level of 0.5x.
• TCL (000100.SZ, Derrick Yang): We are EW with a price target of Rmb4.70 (1.7x
2026e P/B). The stock is trading at 1.7x 2026e P/B vs. its mid-cycle average of 1.4x
since 2022.
• Greater China DDIC supply chain implications – Novatek (3034.TW, Daniel Yen):
Our Underweight thesis reflects weaker PC, smartphone and automotive demand,

gross margin pressure, and a lack of new growth drivers. That said, Novatek's
OLED DDI roadmap appears more competitive than those of its key Korean peers
in terms of both performance and cost, helping it gain share in Apple's iPhone
supply chain.

Morgan Stanley Research 7
Where We Could Be Wrong
• Stronger end demand: If end demand for major applications turns out to be better
than expected, there could be stronger panel shipments and less panel price
pressure.
• More stringent output control: If major panel makers collectively adjust
utilization to an extreme extent, they could mitigate pricing pressure in the case of
softer demand.
• Non-commodity display business: If the non-commodity business progresses

faster than expected, panel makers could show better financial results.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

產品組合

股價相對大盤走勢

穩懋 (3105 TT)  Buy

PIN 等光通訊訂單效應持續推升業績向上，評等為 Buy。  
投資建議 ：穩懋 08/2026 營收 19.37 億元， MoM+2.40% ，符合
群益先前預估， 3Q26 營收動能來自 optical 、 infrastructure ，預
估 09/2026 營收仍將較 08/2026 增加。就評價而言，穩懋受惠於
PIN 等光通訊新訂單自 06/2026 開始量產，預估出貨量 4Q26 將
持續放大，且後續成長空間可期，為 4Q26 、2027 年重要營收動
能，故群益維持對穩懋 Buy 的投資建議。  
2Q26 稅後淨利 QoQ+83.22% ： 2Q26 營收 52.57 億元，
QoQ+14.54% ，動能來自 cellular 、infrastructure 、optical 等產品，
此外，雖然 optical 新產品量產初期良率較低，不過，由於 2Q26
產品結構優於 1Q26 、產能利用率提高，毛利率提高至 28.24% ，
2Q26 營業利益 QoQ+72.14% ，加上認列 1.44 億元匯兌利益、
2.70 億元投資利益， 2Q26 稅後淨利增加至 9.77 億元，
QoQ+83.22% 。  
就 2Q26 的產品結構而言， cellular 產品營收 QoQ+18.00% ，原
因在於手機業者需求隨逐漸進入旺季而增溫； infrastructure 產
品營收 QoQ+14.53% ，動能來自 AI data center 、低軌道衛星；  
optical 產品 2Q26 重要動能有二， (1) 新款 iPhone 相關 VCSEL
訂單數量增加， (2)PIN 等光通訊新訂單自 06/2026 開始量產。  
PIN 等光通訊新訂單出貨量放大為後續重要營收動能： 展望
3Q26 ， optical 產品受惠於 PIN 等光通訊新訂單出貨量放大，營
收成長性居各產品之冠，預估將帶動 others 產品營收
QoQ+35.97% ，且後續成長空間仍可期；其次， infrastructure 產
品出貨動能來自低軌道衛星標案陸續出貨，預估相關營收
QoQ+20.45% ，由於全球各地低軌道衛星標案隨應用領域拓展而
有機會持續釋出，後續營收前景亦仍佳。  
至於 cellular 、 Wi-Fi 產品出貨成長性雖受到近期記憶體缺料等
因素影響整體手機產業景氣而存在不確定性，但現階段預估營
收均與 2Q26 實績相去有限。

2 群益投顧 
  企業 ESG 資訊揭露：  
就企業溫室氣體排放量減量之預算與計畫而言，穩懋持續透過各項節能措
施提升廠內能源使用效率，以減少用電度數，且新設產線全面採用最佳可行
技術，實際措施包括 PFCs 尾氣處理設備全面採用去除效率達 90% 以上之型
式 (IPCC 公告指定型式 )、逐步汰換既設產線之 PFCs 尾氣處理設備、評估製
程氣體減量使用或取代高潛勢溫室氣體使用、採取工程改善或設備更換以
降低用電量、評估新建廠房設置廠內再生能源等。  
再就企業產品或服務帶給客戶或消費者之減碳效果而言，穩懋致力於新技
術的開發，持續推出高效能的技術平台，以生產更具能源效益和節能的半導
體通訊產業產品，因終端產品應用逐漸擴及手機、無線網路、雷達、衛星等
各個通訊領域，有助於全球能源節省和減少用電消耗。此外，藉由穩懋多功
能的晶片整合、製程線寬的縮減以及製程能力的提升，終端產品之尺寸、單
位晶片使用量均持續減少，亦得以為守護地球環境盡一份心力。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

日月光投控 (3711 TT)  Buy

高階封測需求持續吃緊，建議 Buy。  
投資建議 ：AI 與 HPC 帶動的先進封測與測試需求成長快速，日
月光投控再度上調先進封測 (LEAP) 營收 ， 將超越先前設定35 億
美元目標， 2027 年 LEAP 營收有望再增加 1 倍；一般封測成長
率預估由先前 YoY+13% 上修至 YoY+20% 。整 體ATM 業務 2026
年營收預期 YoY+35% 。 EMS 營收略低於 YoY+20% 。預估日月
光投控 2026 年稅後 EPS 17.95 元。全球超大規模資料中心正加
速升級，為支撐具備多模態能力的複雜推論，晶片設計已大量
轉向由多顆小晶片與高頻寬記憶體堆疊的系統架構，使台積電
CoWoS 產能無不應求。日月光受惠台積電訂單外溢，加上大客
戶 AMD 的次世代 Venice CPU 架構開始放量，日月光的 FOCoS
出貨向上，將帶動營收續創新高，因此對日月光投控投資建議
為 Buy ，目標價為 862 元 (2027 年 PER35X) 。  
2Q26 稅後 EPS 4.71 元：日月光投控公布日月光投控 2Q26 營收
1,910.64 億元， QoQ+10.02% 、YoY+26.74% 。其中 2Q26 ATM 營
收 1,261.48 億元， QoQ+12.19% 、 YoY+36.28% 。優於日月光預
期 2Q26 IC ATM 營收 QoQ+9 ~+11% 。合併毛利率方面，產能利
用率還是主要影響毛利率的因素。日月光投控整體合併毛利率
增加 0.94 個百分點至 21.01% 。 2Q26 稅後 EPS 4.71 元。  
日月光預估 2026 年資本支出達 105 億美元左右： 高階測試產能
非常吃緊，配合客戶的需求，日月光投控再度增加 2026 年資本
支出 20 億美元，約達 105 億美元，其中 40 億美元將用於新廠
房建設 65 億美元用於設備， YoY+91% 左右。設備支出中，大約
56% 用於封裝， 40% 用於測試，其餘用於 EMS 和材料。整體而
言，大約 70% 的設備資本支出是用於先進技術，大部分將分配
給 LEAP ，特別是晶圓測試。  
折舊方面，雖部分折舊到期，但資本支出大量增加，日月光投
控預計 2026 年折舊費用 YoY+12~15% ，折舊費用將達到約新台
幣 750 億元以上。

2 群益投顧 
  日月光 ESG ：  
永續浪潮不斷， ESG 成為企業競爭力的關鍵，日月光擴大台灣先進封測布局
的同時，更持續耕耘與扎根企業社會責任，致力於發展全方位的綠色概念。
永續治理：日月光集團是負責任的企業公民，以積極的行動確保商業行為符
合專業及道德的最高標準。公司長期的經營與成功必須仰賴企業的永續發
展及其社會責任的體現。  
環境與綠色創新：日月光承諾持續增加資源再利用及減少溫室氣體與廢水
排放、廢棄物產生及化學品使用，以改善生態效益並保護環境。  
供應鏈發展：日月光秉持「積極、互信、誠正、自省」的核心價值追求持續
成長，對於供應鏈管理日月光與時俱進，從選商、運用策略執行採購品項、
供應商整合，到集結各領域資源建構平台與集團採購制度標準化，更將永續
供應落實到採購日常管理，規範供應夥伴遵守行為準則，以「價值導向」引
導供應商重視永續發展，促進產業創新。  
員工關懷與發展：日月光集團承諾提供給員工安全、健康及優質的工作環
境，確保人才多元化及保障人權，並使公司擁有高技能及經驗豐富的人力資
本。  
社會參與：日月光集團積極參與慈善行動、教育計畫和社會工作回饋社區，
將資源做最完善的分配，並發揮正面的社會影響力。日月光集團的社會參與
策略係以「環保公益」、「產學教育」、「社區營造」及「對外倡議」為四
大發展面向，於「集團永續發展委員會」下設置了「社會參與團隊 (Social 
Involvement Taskforce) 」來促動集團內社會參與相關行動，並依循 LBG 
(London Benchmark Group) 模式審視各個發展面向之相關投入、效益及其影
響，每半年進行業務績效檢視及報告。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

yizhen0808@yuanta.com

彰濱廠與嘉興廠需求回升帶動產能滿載，3Q26 獲利延續成長 
2Q26 台灣營收季增 23%、中國營收季增 56%，受惠台灣半導體先進製程及
化學品供應鏈擴廠需求延續，台灣廠既有產能維持滿載；中國半導體及記憶
體廠擴產亦帶動桶槽設備拉貨回升，嘉興廠稼動率提升至 95%-100%，EPS
季增 23.4%至 3.11 元。3Q26 隨台灣擴產效益逐步顯現、稼動率提升及訂單
組合改善，預估毛利率季增 0.7ppts 至 36.3%，惟仍受先前承接的低毛利訂
單影響，改善幅度相對溫和，EPS 估季增 29.3%至 4.02 元。 
訂單能見度至 2028 年，備貨與擴產支撐 2026-2027 年成長 
觀察上品 1) 3Q26 在手訂單由年初 38 億回升至 75 億台幣，接單動能明顯轉
強；2) 2Q26 存貨季增 21.5%至 20.52 億元，其中商品及製成品季增 24.7% 
(佔比提升至 44%)，反應部分訂單已完成生產，目前正進行客戶廠內驗收，
待驗收完成並依約交貨後，才陸續認列營收，且存貨周轉天數未伴隨備貨明
顯惡化。本輪訂單、備貨及產能利用率同步回升，配合 1H27 產能排程已滿
與擴產規劃，訂單能見度到 2028 年，且台灣/中國/美國晶圓/化學品廠積極
擴廠計畫，我們認為將支持 2H26-2027 年營收與獲利成長，估 2026/2027
年 EPS 將年增 39.3%/52.8%至 14.21/21.72 元。 
給予上品買進評等，目標價 350 元 
中國市場雖仍面臨價格競爭，需求回升已帶動嘉興廠滿載，台灣晶圓廠與化
學品廠亦持續擴產，我們認為上品憑藉氟樹脂加工、設備整合及客戶認證優
勢，可望掌握建廠商機，持續支持營收與獲利成長。評價方面， 參考上品過
去三年本益比多落在 11-19 倍、同業平均本益比約 13-18 倍，考量上品具較
佳的獲利能力與 2027 年獲利成長潛力，且訂單能見度至 2028 年，我們認
為目前評價尚未充分反應擴產及毛利率修復效益，因此給予 16 倍本益比，
以 2027 年 EPS 21.72 元，推得目標價 350 元，給予買進評等。 
元大觀點 
⧫  
3Q26 台灣半導體先進製程及化學品供應鏈擴廠需求延續、中國半導體
及記憶體廠擴產亦帶動拉貨回升，彰濱/嘉興稼動率皆滿載。 
⧫  
在手訂單由年初 38 億回升至 75 億台幣，接單動能明顯轉強；2Q26 存
貨季增 22%，其中商品及製成品季增 25%，營收將陸續貢獻。 
⧫  
考量上品具較佳的獲利能力與 2027 年獲利成長潛力，且訂單能見度至
2028 年，以 2027 年 EPS、16 倍本益比，推得目標價 350 元。

在手訂單與備貨同步回升，支撐 2H26-2027 年營收成長 
觀察上品 1) 3Q26 在手訂單由年初 38 億回升至 75 億台幣，接單動能明顯轉強；2) 2Q26 存貨季增
21.5%至 20.52 億元，其中商品及製成品季增 24.7% ( 佔比提升至 44%)，反應部分訂單已完成生產，
目前正進行客戶廠內驗收，待驗收完成並依約交貨後，才陸續認列營收； 存貨周轉天數則由 1Q26
的 214 天下降至 203 天，與 2025 年同期約 202 天相近，顯示備貨增加尚未伴隨周轉明顯惡化。相
較 2023 年客戶拉貨遞延、製成品堆積且周轉天數突破 300 天，本輪訂單、備貨及產能利用率同步
回升，配合 1H27 產能排程已滿與擴產規劃，我們認為將支持 2H26-2027 年營收成長。以下分別

稼動率回升與訂單組合改善，推動 2026-2027 年獲利逐步修復 
上品毛利率於 2024-2025 年受價格競爭、產品組合轉差及產能利用率下降影響而承壓，2026 年仍
受前一年承接的低毛利台灣訂單拖累，相關訂單預計交付至 2027 年初。若依期完成，隨較佳定價
的新訂單陸續認列，毛利率壓力有望逐步緩解。觀察 1) 2Q26 彰濱既有產能已滿載、嘉興稼動率回
升至 95%-100%，隨後續 貨認列，產能利用率改善效益有望逐步反映於營收與獲利； 2) 在手訂
單回升至 75 億元，其中約 80%來自毛利率相對較高的台灣業務，後續交付有望帶動地區組合改善。

此外，材料成本主要受 PTFE/PFA 以及白鐵價格影響，公司採 專案報價，依接單當時的材料、人
工及製造成本估算，並預留成本波動空間，同時透過與供應商協調、提前安排採購，以及調整新訂
單報價，降低原料漲價衝擊。上品直接向上游原料廠採購，亦具相對有利的貨源取得條件，然設備
接單至交付存在時間差，若既有訂單售價已確定、後續採購成本持續上升，仍可能壓縮專案毛利，
因此隨新訂單逐步反應最新成本，材料漲價壓力有望緩解 。另外，先前已提列跌價的存貨，若淨變
現價值隨材料價格回升，亦有機會認列回沖利益，提供額外獲利貢獻 。整體而言，我們認為 2026
年營運已進入修復階段， 2027 年則有望更完整反映新增產能及訂單組合改善效益，支持獲利持續

垂直整合與高純度材料加工能力，強化上品競爭優勢與獲利韌性 
華爾卡為上品在氟樹脂內襯槽體領域的主要競爭對手之一 。觀察雙方相關業務，上品具較高的半導
體供應鏈曝險 (上品半導體比重約 90% vs 華爾卡約 30-40%)，且設備、管路及材料合計營收規模
已高於華爾卡機能樹脂部門。2025 年需求偏弱及同業低價競爭雖壓抑上品獲利 (圖 15)，在 2026 
年原物料成本上升環境下，仍具維持獲利的韌性。 
我們認為上品競爭優勢在於：1) 具備由氟樹脂原料加工、內襯板材與管材製造，延伸至高純度化學
品設備設計及安裝的整合能力，相較同業多只生產設備，零件材料採外購方式；2) 布局改質 PTFE、
PFA/PFA HP 等材料加工技術，因應客戶對低污染、抗滲透與設備可靠性的要求；3) 直接向主要原
料廠採購及既有客戶認證，有助提高供貨穩定性與接單機會。隨高規格產品比重提升，我們認為上
品可憑藉材料加工、污染控制及客戶認證累積的技術門檻，強化產品差異化與客戶黏著度，使客戶
採購更重視品質、交付能力及長期使用成本，有助降低單純價格競爭對接單與獲利的影響。  
圖 15：上品營益率 續高於華爾卡樹脂部門 圖 16：上品營收規模大於華爾卡，且半導體比
重較高

資料來源：公司資料、元大投顧 資料來源：公司資料、元大投顧 
訂單回升與擴產效益逐步顯現，2027 年獲利成長可期 
我們認為上品 2026 年營運已進入復甦階段，2027 年將反映擴產與訂單組合改善效益。受惠台灣先
進製程擴廠及電子化學品供應鏈持續投資，公司在手訂單由年初約 38 億元增至 75 億元，後續仍有
成長空間。目前 1H27 產能排程已滿，部分訂單交期延伸至 2028 年，有助支撐未來營收成長，預
估 2027 年台灣/中國地區營收年增 29.2%/17.1%。 
本中心估上品 2026/2027 年營收將年增 19.8%/29.4%至 56.6 億/73.3 億元，主要來自內襯設備產
能 擴 充 、 稼 動 率 提 升 ， 以 及 管 件 與 相 關 工 程 需 求 增 加 。 估 毛 利 率 年 增3.1ppts/3.7ppts 至
36.1%/39.8%，2027 年改善主要受惠低價訂單逐步消化及設備毛利率回升 ，且毛利率相對較高的
台灣業務比重增加，在手訂單後續交付有望帶動地區組合改善提升毛利 ；營收擴大帶來的規模效益，
有助費用率下降，帶動營益率年增 2.3ppts/5.1ppts 至 23.5%/28.6% 。 估 稅 後 淨 利 年 增
39.3%/52.8%至 11.4 億/17.4 億元，EPS 14.21/21.72 元。

上品 華爾卡

產業概況  
半導體市場 續受惠 AI 強勁需求帶動成長 
2025 年全球半導體受惠 1) HPC 強勁成長；2) PC/ 手機 貨溫和成長；3) AI 在伺服器和消費型產
品的滲透率提升將帶動半導體價值含量增加； 4) 記憶體市場的強勁復甦，整體產業產值年增
21.0%至 7,930 億美元；由於 AI 需求持續強勁、近期記憶體市場受惠 AI 推論需求大幅成長，預估
2026 年全球半導體產值將持續年增 21%至 9,600 億美元。 
圖 17： 2026 年全球半導體營收預估年增 21%至 9,600 億美元

資料來源：Gartner、Digitimes、元大投顧 
半導體產能擴充和製程演進，將 續推升特用化學品用量 
全球半導體材料市場和半導體市場具有高度關聯，根據 TECHCET 資料，2026 年全球半導體材料
市場營收將年增 9%，2024-2029 年 CAGR 達 4.5%， AI 晶片帶來的需求成長外，新產能持續的
擴充和整體半導體產業的技術革新都將成為推升半導體材料市場的動能。其中濕式 /氣體化學品市
場在 2024-2029 年以約 6%/5%的 CAGR 成長，增 略為高於整體材料產業，占半導體材料市場比
重將由原先的 16%提升至 18%，我們認為推升特用化學品占半導體材料市場比重的動力，主要在
於晶片自身設計複雜化和半導體製程的微縮：隨著製成演進光罩層數 斷增加(由 7nm 的 4-5 層增
加至 3nm 的 23-25 層)，同步帶動半導體製作過程中工序和層數的增加、整個晶圓製造過程中，反
覆的清洗、曝光、蝕刻等工藝流程，均需要特用化學品進行相關處理，使各式 化學品品項和用量持
續提升，而隨著線寬微縮所帶來的高精確度要求，亦看到製造廠商對化學品的精確度、平滑度、耐
熱度、潔淨度等等品質提 更高的標準，並對化學品要求具有更高的潔淨度。  
圖 18：全球半導體材料市場 2024-2029 年 CAGR 達 4.5%

全球半導體供應鏈在地化趨勢，將使台系特化業者潛在市場和滲透率雙雙提升 
自 2019 年起經歷疫情、美中科技戰、地緣政治等變化影響，各國意識到自建完整半導體供應鏈能
帶來提高自主性、安全性和可 控性，紛紛提 各項 助和扶植政策，將半導體產業從原本的全球化、
供應鏈功能協作，朝向多區域、多生態競爭發展，我們認為半導體供應鏈在地化的趨勢對台系業者
影響分為兩個面向： 1) 建置更多區域化半導體產能，將提升既有半導體供應鏈潛在市場； 2) 台系
半導體廠積極推動供應鏈在地化，使本土供應鏈廠商滲透率提升。 
1) 建置更多區域化半導體產能，將提升既有半導體供應鏈潛在市場 
台灣和韓國為世界高度依賴的半導體製造據點，然在前段提及原因影響下，政府的激勵政策和 貼
措施促使中國、美國和日本等國家進行大規模產能建置：美國政府的 貼政策及客戶邀約驅使台積
電、三星和 Intel 等半導體大廠在當地進行先進製程佈局，台積電在川普政府要求下於日前宣布將
在美國投資 1,650 億美元建造晶圓廠，TrendForce 預估美國先進製程代工產能市佔率將由 2023 年
12%上升至 2030 年 22%、中國雖然在先進製程發展遭遇阻力，但在國家政策、大基金和內需市場
的推動提升中國的晶片國產化能力下，成熟製程和記憶體產能發展  ， TrendForce 預估中國成
熟製程產能市佔率將由 2023 年 31%提升至 2027 年 39%、同時眾多半導體代工廠前往日本設立合
資廠，均為各國建置區域化的半導體產能的實例，使台灣和韓國半導體代工產能市佔率於 2027 年
下滑至 41%/10%。

2) 台系半導體廠 極推動供應鏈在地化，本土供應鏈廠商滲透率提升 
我國經濟部長表示半導體攸 關國安及經濟安全，隨著先進製程及封裝需求 斷擴大，將協助國內業
者開發半導體關鍵設備、零組件及材料，目標於 2028 年先進封裝設備自主率達 25%，半導體材料
自主率達 35%。以台積電為例，為提升供貨彈性、縮短新產品開發時間、減少 必要成本以確保
對客戶的服務品質，自 2019 年起推行供應鏈在地化，扶持在地化設備、耗材供應商，根據其 CSR
報告，2018-2024 年間接原物料在地化採購比例自 48%逐漸提升至 65%，超越原訂 64.9%目標，
並將 2030 年目標調整至 68%。

獲利調整與股票評價
給予上品買進評等，目標價 350 元
2026-2027 年上品受惠台灣先進製程及電子化學品供應鏈持續擴產，在手訂單由年初 38 億元增至
75 億元，1H27 產能排程已滿，部分交期延伸至 2028 年，中期營收能見度提升。隨低毛利舊訂單
預計於 2027 年初交付完畢、較佳定價新單陸續認列，毛利率有望改善。中國市場雖仍面臨價格競
爭，需求回升已帶動嘉興廠滿載，台灣晶圓廠與化學品廠亦持續擴產，我們認為上品憑藉氟樹脂加
工、設備整合及客戶認證優勢，可望掌握建廠商機，持續支持營收與獲利成長。
評價方面，參考上品過去三年本益比多落在 11-19 倍、同業平均本益比約 13-18 倍，考量上品具較
佳的獲利能力與 2027 年獲利成長潛力，且訂單能見度至 2028 年，我們認為目前評價尚未充分反
應擴產及毛利率修復效益，因此給予 16 倍本益比，以 2027 年 EPS 21. 72 元，推得目標價 350 元，

給予買進評等。

公司簡介  
上品於 1981 年成立，該公司配合 IC 及 TFT-LCD 產業對電子級化學品嚴苛的品質需求，建構各等級的無塵室，提供各種氟
素樹脂成型品，採用 PTFE、PFA、FEP、ETFE、PVDF 等材料，提供塗層(coating)、鋼鐵業酸洗槽(Tank lining) 、石化工
業鐵氟龍管路系統及零件(pipe & fitting lining) 、射 零件(injection parts) 、擠型(Extrusion)等產品，擁有國內氟素樹脂最多
種類的成型技術，也獲得最多國內外專利。上品工廠位於桃園及彰濱，強大的研發及工程團隊，可提供 IC、TFT-LCD 及化
工工廠各種廠務端設備、製程設備及零件、運送槽車、工程規劃與施工等。相較其他公司產品用途較為單一，上品競爭優
勢在於研發鐵氟龍的應用端，自製率高達 80-90%，可望開創新應用、新市場。

ESG 分析  
分別藉由「在 ESG 議題上的曝險」和「個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力」兩個面向來衡量公司於 ESG 上的表現。 
► ESG 總分： 上品整體的 ESG 風險評級屬於低風險，於 Sustainalytics 資料庫所覆蓋的公司中排名亦排行於中間偏前的
位置，且在泛用化學品行業中的公司中排名略優於同業。 
► 在 ESG 議題上的曝險： 上品的整體曝險屬於中等水準，與泛用化學品行業的平均水準相近。公司較需要改善的  ESG 
議題包含商業道德以及排放、廢水和廢棄物問題。 
► 個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力：上品在針對重大 ESG 議題的管理水準和執行力屬中等。該公司的資訊揭露力度很大，
顯示該公司對投資者和公眾高度負責。 它有一些舉措來管理與重大 ESG 議題相關的風險，但該公司缺乏關鍵領域的政
策和計劃。 儘管如此，該公司並未捲入任何與 ESG 相關的重大爭議。

投資評等說明 
買進：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持正面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中增持該股。 
持有-超越同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力高於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
持有-落後同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力低於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
賣 ：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持負面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中減持該股。 
評估中：本中心之預估、評等、目標價尚在評估中，但仍積極追蹤該個股。 
限制評等：為遵循相關法令規章及/或元大之政策，暫 給予評等及目標價。

註：元大給予個股之目標價係依 12 個月投資期間計算。大中華探索系列報告並無正式之 12 個月目標價，其投資建議乃根據分析師報告中之指定期間分析
而得。

休閒車新訂單與電子新產品挹注成長 
 2H26 持續受惠美國 Honda 新訂單放量出貨 ， 以及越南電動機車客戶
產能供不應求的外包訂單需求 ， 營收可望逐季墊高 。 推估下半年平均
每月營收 8~8.5 億元。 
 休閒車產品 2026 年在客戶 Polaris 調整告一段落以及北美 Honda 與
哈雷新訂單開始挹注下，預期營收貢獻將成長 50%以上，2027 年北
美 Honda 還有其他車型將量產，推估可再成長 20-30%。 
 相關機櫃與散熱產品將於 2Q26 陸續少量出貨 ，2027 年將放量出貨 。
預估電子產品營收佔比年底可增至 3-5%。因應電子與大型自動化產
線需求越南百善二廠二期擴建，預計 3Q27 末完工，推估 2027 年營
收比重可達近雙位數。 
 展望 2026 年，受惠越南市場受惠發展電動二輪政策，以及受惠北美
Honda 與哈雷新訂單挹注。預期 2026 年 EPS 17.25 元。2027 年休
閒車新訂單挹注以及電子產品隨著產能開出放量 ， 預期EPS 21.3 元。 
 維持買進 ：公司受惠北美 HONDA 新訂單與電動機車客戶轉單挹
注，今明兩年營運轉好。此外，受惠供應鏈由中國轉移至越南，公司
越南廠具在地優勢取得電子新訂單 ， 隨著越南百善二廠廠能開出 ， 將
挹注未來成長動能。維持買進建議，目標價 234 元。

個股報告

北美新訂單挹注，營運逐季墊高

越南電動二輪政策需求， 彌補燃
油機車缺口

公司 2Q26 營收 24.1 億元 ，+16.9%QoQ，+53.1%YoY， 主要為休閒車輛訂單回溫 ，
加上越南機車業務拉貨回升。在稼動率提升與產品結構轉好下，毛利率23.8%較上
季好轉，單季 EPS 4.42 元。

2H26 持續受惠美國 Honda 新訂單放量出貨 ， 以及越南電動機車客戶產能供不應求
的外包訂單需求 ， 營收可望逐季墊高 。 推估下半年平均每月營收8~8.5 億元 ，3Q26
營收 24.34 億元，+1%QoQ，+58.8%YoY，EPS 4.72 元。4Q26 為越南機車產業
旺季，預計營收 25.07 億元，+3%QoQ，+38.9%YoY，EPS 4.53 元。

1H26 主要產品營收佔比依序為機車 44.8%、 休閒車43.9%、 健康8.6%、 汽車2.6%。

機車產品方面，越南傳統機車市場為主，主要客戶為 YAMAHA 與 Honda，2025
年受到 HONDA 下修生產計劃影響，營收貢獻較前一年下滑 14%。2026 年越南河
內與胡志明市將禁止傳統燃油二輪，轉為發展電動二輪市場，雖影響傳統燃油機車
市場，但受惠政策，新的電動二輪換車需求彌補了油車下滑的缺口。  
目前客戶 Vinfast 產能吃緊釋出訂單 ，1H26 機車產品營收貢獻成長 22%， 預期2026
年機車營收可成長 16-17%。2027 年越南 Honda 將有電動二輪產品推出，有利維
持機車產品成長。

北美 Honda 與哈雷為休閒車產品
主要成長動能

汽車與健康事業轉佳

休閒車：主要客戶為 Polaris、Harley 與北美 Honda，受惠客戶為降低成本與避開
當地關稅的策略，將產線移至越南，以及美國當地非紅供應鏈政策，相關供應鏈由
中國轉向越南。而 2025 年在川普總統推動美國製造下，公司調整產線並於美國南
卡羅來納州設廠因應。2025 年受到通膨影響北美休閒車市場需求趨緩，主要客戶
Polaris 調整庫存，目前庫存水位已將至低位，自 1Q26 客戶陸續補庫存。

新訂單方面，北美 Honda ATV 零件，目前接單規劃至 2028 年都有新專案出貨。
由於 Honda ATV 在北美規模與 Polaris 相當，為未來成長的主要客戶與動能，新
訂單於 2Q26 量產，預估營收貢獻將從 2025 年的 4.9 億元大幅增至 2026 年的 
12~13 億元。此外，公司哈雷訂單於 1Q26 開始出貨，推估 2026 年可貢獻 9-10
億元，目前仍有新項目在開發，將挹注未來營運動能。

休閒車產品 2026 年在客戶 Polaris 調整告一段落以及北美 Honda 與哈雷新訂單開
始挹注下，預期營收貢獻將成長 50%以上。2027 年除了北美 Honda SXS 與 ATV
訂單外，還有其他車型將量產，加上哈雷，與歐洲、日本的新訂單，推估休閒車營
收貢獻可再成長 20-30%。

汽車相關產品取得 Toyota、Honda 與 Vinfast 電動車訂單，加上其他車廠未來也
規劃將組裝線設於越南， 公司看好未來越南與東協車用市場成長， 透過與相關供應
鏈的台商合資取得供應商資格，未來將可從業外認列轉投資收益。 2025 年受到越
南需求不如預期影響 ， 營收貢獻僅成長6%，2026 年越南車用市場回穩 ， 加上Vinfast

產線需求越南百善二廠二期擴建，預計 3Q27 末完工，推估 2027 年營收比重可達
近雙位數。

營收走勢圖

研究員/張均壕 JUN_HAO1@uni-psg.com

由於北美 Honda 新車型訂單貢獻顯著，帶動 3Q26 營收淡季不淡，加上
電子新業務的訂單開始貢獻，為豐祥下半年營運挹注強勁成長動能， 研
究部據此上修豐祥今年獲利 8%，預估2026 年 EPS 17.17 元(+41%YOY)。
展望 2027 年，預期在休閒車輛、電動機車新訂單以及電子新業務貢獻
下，豐祥明年業績將繼續攀高，預估 2027 年 EPS 21.09 元。研究部給
予 12 倍本益比，將目標價上調至 250 元，維持買進評等。

重點摘要：

1. 電子業務啟動第二成長曲線，2027年進入量產階段： 
豐祥將電子業務視為企業新一輪發展的核心驅動力 ， 當前以AI
伺服器機櫃為主力產品以及其他電子相關品項。 今年下半年已
陸續有新產品少量出貨。在營收比重部分，2026年電子業務約
占合併營收的3~5%。未來隨著客戶需求釋放及新項目量產，搭
配新廠房啟用，創造更多生產空間，公司規畫2H27開始以大規
模自動化模式提升產能， 預期2027年電子業務將有更多的營收
貢獻，更能增添中長期營運動能。。

2. 3Q26淡季不淡，4Q26營收再攀高，年度獲利上修： 
豐祥第三季繼續受惠北美Honda新專案出貨順暢，季度營收 表
現優於原先季減的預期， 呈現淡季不淡 ， 延續前季的強勁表現 ，
年增率保持大幅成長 ； 之後進入越南當地電動機車零件拉貨旺
季，第四季營收有望再度衝高。整體而言，在休閒車輛與電動
機車業務雙引擎帶動下，公司下半年營收動能無虞，本業獲利
維持在高檔區域並保有顯著的年成長動能 ， 加上轉投資事業獲
利成長，研究部上修豐祥今年度獲利9%，預估豐祥2026年合併
營收94.68億元(+39%YOY)，毛利率23.4%(+1.0pct)，費用率
9.4%(+0.8pct)， 預估稅後淨利11.94億元(+41%YOY)， 預估2026
年EPS 17.17元。

3. 三大成長引擎接棒，2027年業績將再創高： 
展望2027年，研究部認為豐祥將繼續保有偏強的營運動能，主
要來自於三大核心領域 ：(1)休閒車輛新訂單持續堆疊與放量 。
特別是受惠於北美供應鏈轉移效益 ， 豐祥對客戶的供應滲透率
提升，北美Honda、Harley-Davidson等客戶陸續有新機種將於
明年量產；亦有日系客戶的新訂單將提前至明年出貨，使休閒
車業務在2027年依然是貢獻整體營收增長的最主要基石；

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

(2)越南當地電動機車與電動汽車市場規模擴大，相關訂單持
續增加，為既有業務提供成長支撐 ；(3)電子新業務的實質貢
獻。豐祥切入伺服器機櫃供應鏈，經過2026年下半年的試產準
備後，2027年將迎來訂單放量，預期電子業務佔營收比重躍升
至5%以上。此外，休閒車輛占比提高，產品組合將繼續優化，
毛利率能再墊高；配合物流逐漸回歸自營管理，費用率亦有望
下降，將進一步提升盈利能力。研究部預估豐祥2027年合併營
收116.46 億元(+23%YOY) ，毛 利 率23.7%(+0.3pct) ，費 用 率
9.1%(-0.3pct)， 預估稅後淨利14.70億元(+23%YOY)， 預估2027
年EPS 21.09元。

附錄 一 ： 評 等 之 標 準 
評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下  
未評等  沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等

附 錄 二 ： 免 責 宣 言 
© 2010 統 一 投 資 顧 問 股 份 有 限 公 司 版 權 所 有 。 本 公 司 提 供 之 報 告 內 容 係 根 據 本 公
司 認 可 之 資 料來 源 ， 並 基 於 特 定 日 期 所 做 之 判 斷 ， 但 不 保 證 其 完 整 性 或 正 確 性 ， 報
告 中 所 有 的 意 見 及 預 估 ， 如 有 變 更 恕 不 另 行 通 知 。

本 研 究 報 告 所 載 之 投 資 資 訊 ， 僅 提 供 客 戶 做 為 一 般 投 資 參 考 ， 並 非 針 對 特 定 對 象 提
供 專 屬 之 投 資 建 議 。 文 中 所 載 資 訊 或 任 何 意 見 ， 不 構 成 任 何 買 賣 有 價 證 券 或 其 他 投
資 標 的 之 要 約 、 宣 傳 或 引 誘 等 事 項 。 對 於 本 投 資 報 告 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 任 何 證 券 、 投
資 標 的 ， 或 文 中 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 投 資 策 略 ， 投 資 人 應 就 其 是 否 適 合 本 身 財 務 狀 況 與
投 資 條 件 ， 進 一 步 諮 詢 財 務 顧 問 的 意 見 。 本 投 資 報 告 之 內 容 取 材 自 據 信 為 可 靠 之 資
料 來 源 ， 但 概 不 以 明 示 或 默 示 的 方 式 ， 對 資 料 之 準 確 性 、 完 整 性 或 正 確 性 作 出 任 何
陳 述 或 保 證 。 本 投 資 報 告 載 述 意 見 進 行 更 改 與 撤 回 並 不 另 行 通 知 。 本 投 資 報 告 並 非
（且不應 解 釋 為 ）在 任 何 司 法 管 轄 區 內 ， 任 何 非 依 法 從 事 證 券 經 紀 或 交 易 之 人 士 或
公 司 ， 為 於 該 管 轄 區 內 從 事 證 券 經 紀 或 交 易 之 遊 說 。 本 投 資 報 告 內 容 屬 統 一 投 顧 之
著 作 權 ， 嚴 禁 抄 襲 與 仿 造 。

### Page 1

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 29 日

1 群益投顧 
世界先進 (5347 TT) 和 恩 智 浦 半 導 體(NXP Semiconductors N.V.)  合資公司 VSMC 第
一座 12 廠開幕 。VSMC 第一座廠的訂單已滿 ， 且規劃第二座中 。 由於目前 AI 與 HPC
電源管理應用 需 求 佳， 世 界 先 進獲 利 逐 步 向 上 ， 惟 產 能 瓶 頸 不 利 短 期 成 長 動 能 ，且達
目標價， 因 此 建 議 Neutral 。

研 究 員 ： 陳 俐 妍   
ly.c@capital.com.tw

世 界 先 進 和 恩 智 浦 半 導 體 NXP 成 立 合 資 公 司 VSMC 第 一 座 12 廠開幕 ：  
09/28/2026 VSMC 位 於 新 加 坡 淡 濱 尼 的 首 座 12 吋 晶 圓 廠 舉 行 開 幕 典 禮 。 新
廠自 12/2024 動 土 ， 歷 時 22 個 月 完 工 ， 首 批 試 產 晶 圓 良 率 超 過 99% ， 預 計
1Q27 量 產 。 台 積 電 廠 務 副 總 經 理 莊 子 壽 、 帆 宣 董 事 長 高 新 明 、 應 用 材 料 台
灣 區 總 裁 余 定 陸 等 人 到 場 祝 賀 。 
VSMC 蓋 廠 速 度 優 於 預 期， 二 廠 規劃中 ：  
06/05/2024 世 界 先 進 和 恩 智 浦 半 導 體 NXP 宣 布 於 新 加 坡 共 同 成 立 Vision 
Power Semiconductor Manufacturing Company (VSMC) 合 資 公 司 ， 以 興 建 一
座 12 吋 (300mm) 晶 圓 廠 。 晶 圓 廠 將 建 置 40~130nm 製 程 ， 生 產 包 括 混 合 訊
號 、 電 源 管 理 和 類 比 相 關 產 品 ， 以 支 援 汽 車 、 工 業 、 消 費 性 電 子 及 行 動 裝 置
等 終 端 市 場 的 需 求 ， 大 都 是 歐 美 客 戶 ， 也 有 很 多 新 客 戶 ，2H24 開始興建首
座 晶 圓 廠 ，2H26 有 Engineering Sample 產 出 ，2027 年 ramp up 。 此 合 資 公 司
VSMC 為 一 家 獨 立 的 晶 圓 製 造 服 務 廠 商 ， 為 合 作 夥 伴 雙 方 提 供 一 定 比 例 的 產
能。  
恩 智 浦 執 行 長 Rafael Sotomayor 表 示，VSMC 晶 圓 廠 是 NXP 自 2015 年 收 購
Freescale 以 來 ， 近 11 年 來 最 大 的 製 造 端 投 資。 汽 車 與 工 業 加 速 轉 向 智 慧 邊
緣 運 算，客 戶 對 供 貨 韌 性 極 度 焦 慮，這 座 新 廠為 恩 智 浦 提供 車 用 和 電 源 管 理
晶片供貨 的新 基 地 。  
VSMC 新廠自 2024 年 底 動 土 至 設 備 進 駐 ， 僅 花 費20.5 個 月 便 完 工 ， 刷 新 新
加 坡 晶 圓 廠 建 廠 最 快 紀 錄 ， 首 批 晶 片 於2026 年 中 下 線 ， 最 大 功 臣 正 是 台 積
電 的 全 力 奧 援，除 技 術 移 轉 130 nm 至 40 nm 製 程 及 矽 中 介 層 技 術，台 積 電 廠
務 副 總 經 理 莊 子 壽 更 率 領 廠 務 團 隊 進 駐，將 台 灣 成 熟 的 建 廠 實 力 整 套 輸 出，
排 除 各 項 工 程 難 題。莊 子 壽 透 露，新 加 坡 沒 有 地 震，因 此 再 蓋 廠 設 計 無 需 加
入 防 震 ， 設 計 上 比 較 簡 單 ， 若 要 蓋 二 廠 只 需 要18 個 月 就 可 以 ， 但 因 現 有 土
地 的 限 制 只 能 再 蓋 一 座 。 
世 界 先 進 董 事 長 方 略 指 出，一 廠 產 能 已 被 客 戶 預 訂 完 畢，新 增 需 求 須 等 待 二
廠 供 應 ， 因 此 公 司 已 著 手 評 估 Fab 2 時 程 。 二 廠 啟 動 前 ， 須 確 認 財 務 規 畫 、
客 戶 需 求 及 技 術 定 位 。12 吋 晶 圓 廠 投 資 金 額 龐 大 ， 若 製 程 定 位 錯 誤 ， 日 後
可 能 面 臨 結 構 性 問 題；即 使 目 前 已 聽 到 客 戶 提 出 需 求，仍 須 審 慎 評 估 後 才 能
決 定 投 資 時 點 與 規 模 。 
二 廠 製 程 技 術 方 面 ，22nm 及 28nm 都 在 考 慮 之 中 ， 自 行 研 發 及 技 術 授 權 都
是 可 能 選 項 。 二 廠 仍 將 與 NXP 合 作 ， 不 會 再 引 進 第 三 方 。 相 較 一 廠 ， 二 廠
建 置 難 度 可 望 降 低 ， 包 括 部 分 廠 務 及 建 築 等 基 礎 設 施 已 完 成 ，Fab 2 啟 動 時
程 可 望 加 快 腳 步 。 
受 惠 於 客 戶 寄 託 設 備 ， 使 資 本 支 出 需 求 降 低 ， 該 座12 吋 廠 的 總 投 資 金 額 由
原 先 計 畫 的 78 億 美 元 下 修 為 67 億 美 元 ， 月 產 能 亦 下 修 至 44K ：

### Page 2

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 29 日

2 群益投顧 
受 惠 於 客 戶 寄 託 設 備 ， 使 資 本 支 出 需 求 降 低 ， 該 座12 吋 廠 的 總 投 資 金 額 由
原 先 計 畫 的 78 億 美 元 下 修 為 67 億 美 元。股 權 結 構 維 持 不 變，世 界 先 進 出 資
24 億美元 ( 持股  60%) ， 恩 智 浦 半 導 體(NXP) 出資 16 億 美 元 (持股  40% )。剩
餘 資 金 將 透 過 客 戶 長 期 產 能 保 證 金 、 使 用 費 、 貸 款 及 政 府 補 貼 來 支 應 。 
新加坡 12 吋 相 關 技 術 授 權 及 技 術 轉 移 來 自 台 積 電 ， 因 為 世 界 先 進 沒 有12 吋
晶圓廠營運的經驗，需要台積電支援。未來此 12 吋 的 廠 將 生 產 中 介 層
(Interposer) 、 混 合 訊 號 、 電 源 管 理 和 類 比 產 品 ， 以 供 應 高 速 運 算 、 汽 車 、 工
業、消 費 性 電 子 及 行 動 裝 置 等 終 端 市 場 的 需 求，產 品 組 合 和 原 先 預 期 有 所 不
同。 主要是 台 積 電 將 矽 中 介 層(Silicon Interposer) 外 包 給 與 世 界 先 進 ， 成 為
VSMC 成 熟 製 程 的 新 需 求 來 源。矽 中 介 層 是 先 進 封 裝 中 的 核 心 元 件 之 一，目
前在 VSMC 規 劃 中 的 占 比 已 相 當 顯 著 ， 但 由 於 相 關 製 程 步 驟 較 多 、 需 要 更
多 設 備 ， 也 因 此 壓 縮 無 塵 室 空 間 ， 進 一 步 限 制 整 體 晶 圓 產 出 。 
原預估 VSMC 2029 年 月 產 能 將 達 55K 12 吋 晶 圓 ， 由 於 產 品 組 合 改 變 和 製
程 更 複 雜 ， 因 此 產 能 下 修 至 44K 12 吋 晶 圓 (無 塵 室 可 用 空 間 皆 滿)， 全 部 都
Sold out ， 也 都 有 長 約 支 持 ，100% 產 能 已 被 預 定 。 
成 熟 製 程 吃 緊：  
世 界 先 進 董 事 長方 略 表 示 AI 投 資 大 幅 增 加 ， 吸 收 大 量 資 金 、 人 才 及 半 導 體
產 能 ， 同 時 對 非 AI 應 用 產 生 明 顯 排 擠 效 應 。 全 球 晶 圓 產 能 吃 緊 也 非 單 一 地
區 現 象，亞 洲、美 洲 及 歐 洲 都 面 臨 供 給 壓 力，預 期 產 能 不 足 將 持 續 很 長 的 時
間 。 這 波 需 求 使 世 界 先 進 同 時 面 臨 既 有8 吋 產 能 吃 緊 及 新 廠 擴 產 評 估 。 
世 界 先 進 既 有 8 吋 產 能 的 利 用 率 狀 況 滿 載 ， 除 傳 統 成 熟 製 程 需 求 外 ， 新 的
AI 相 關 應 用 也 進 一 步 擴 大 成 熟 製 程 需 求，世 界 先 進 在AI Edge 、Smart Power 
System 等 領 域 已 具 備 不 錯 的 位 置，未 來 隨 VSMC 加 入，公 司 在 AI 相 關 市 場
的 布 局 可 望 進 一 步 加 深 。 
AI 帶 來 的 排 擠 效 應 ， 也 讓 原 本 成 熟 製 程 的 供 需 結 構 出 現 明 顯 變 化 。 方 略 表
示，現 階 段 仍 難 以 準 確 預 測 這 波 供 不 應 求 何 時 才 會 出 現 明 顯 緩 解，但 目 公 司
對 2027 年 樂 觀 。 
2026 年 全 球 經 濟 溫 和 成 長，但在 AI 投 資 持 續 擴 大 下，產 業 有 望 迎 來 更 為 健
康 的 成 長 態 勢 。 長 期 而 言 ， 在 AI 全 球 大 投 資 趨 勢 下 ， 半 導 體 在 硬 體 投 資 方
面 扮 演 非 常 重 要 的 角 色 。 對 於 深 耕 電 源 管 理 領 域 的 世 界 先 進 而 言 ， 這 波AI
浪 潮 帶 來 絕 佳 的 機 遇。預 估 世 界 先 進 2026 年 EPS 5.83 元。預 期 12 吋 VSMC
營 運 規 模 提 升 速 度 加 快，且 有 LTA ，達 到 損 平 的 時 間 將 提 前。此 外，成 熟 製
程在 2027 年 有 機 會 再 調 高 報 價 ， 世 界 先 進 在 AI 與 HPC 電 源 管 理 應 用 市 佔
率 提 升 中 ， 獲 利 逐 步 向 上 ， 惟 產 能 瓶 頸 不 利 短 期 成 長 動 能 ，且 達 目 標 價 ，因
此建議 Neutral 。

4 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。
